# Seed 65 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - islands: the fast end 6.8% ahead of seed 64 at the same generation, the small end the best yet (Iteration 449)

`seed 65 12h widths noevolved islands then experiment footprint.py ... then experiment review-checks.py spread` at
e81eec6ff (Iteration 444): the islands trial (results/drafts/escape-mechanism-435.md) - four islands of 64, migrants every
10 generations - free, as seed 64. The remote checks started 13:59:34, the evolution 14:05:33; it evolved 11 h 16 min.
50,281 designs, generations 0-196: 33,328 alive, 16,953 screened (34.5%), none died.

**No verdict** - no comparison step. **The islands trial, against seed 64 (no islands) at its last generation, 146**
(review-checks.py spread, seed 64's database kept): fastest **0.1168 against 0.1253 - 6.8% ahead**; smallest 14,005
against 14,047 (-0.3%); best Q -4.2%; hypervolume 0.6136 against 0.6042 (+1.6%); but at 32 bits its fastest 0.3591
against 0.3326 (+8.0%, slower). At generation 90 (the chain's spread over seeds 61-65): fastest 0.1288 against seed 64's
0.1285, smallest 14,063 against 14,194, hypervolume 0.6272 - the best of the five. The fast end kept gaining: its last
2% at generation 124 (seed 64: 36). **By its end: fastest 0.115, smallest 13,959 bytes - the smallest of any run.**

**Facts.** Its front: 179 designs, 93 with the JIT (jitx and jitloop on all of them); tos on 117. heldout (sieve):
Spearman 0.985, the fastest tenth 0.581, the pick's regret +1.5%. Fast-end optimism 1.115 (the fast gate's rule: revisit
- its deferred second reading applied at this processing). At 32 bits no failure. Rule 7: 27 designs with rtiplus, 10
with the fuser it needs (6% of the front working - its fix applied at this processing). The gene votes over seeds 63-65:
**six candidates - skippad, fold, sharedcall, tail, kstack, tailpn** (12 of 12 each). The footprint check (its 16
fastest): Spearman at 100 and 10,000 words in its pack. Its chain's remote checks, of seed 64's front:
results/xcheck-seed65.md.

---
# Evolved VM designs

33328 designs evaluated, 33328 alive, 16953 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): 8fb19cc40d, speed 0.139, binary + image 28,877, memory 168 KB - Q 0.211 of s6-cv8b's.

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
